git ls-files | grep -v '^\\.claude/skills' | head -80; cat pyproject.toml; cat docs/agents/*.md | head -60"}},"wireIngestContext":{"toolu_01JVj8CrCGUhTG96LrAeDr48":{"cwd":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms"}},"serverClassifierRequest":"31c9b568-8a92-4999-814e-90da1e2e8cc1","apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011Cg31i4b3Vafgg4yeUU3xH","attributionSkill":"implement","type":"assistant","uuid":"3ba7d7ac-7ce3-4eba-adef-252d3ac044c7","timestamp":"2026-10-14T23:40:21.922Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"86c96d58-6503-4970-83eb-16ca927e0436","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"c99fff69-e931-4b00-ba0e-51f2c392c118","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"3ba7d7ac-7ce3-4eba-adef-252d3ac044c7","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"d5cad169-0b32-44c0-b6a6-254151a3c343","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JVj8CrCGUhTG96LrAeDr48","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"body\":\"## Problem Statement\\n\\nThe Jesuit Schools Network has about 60 member high schools. Their alumni news is scattered across social media accounts and buried on each school's own news page, so alumni connect with classmates but not with the wider network. Each school has one person (usually the head of alumni relations) who writes about their school. The network director, Jennifer LaMaster, supervises but does not write day to day. She needs one place where every School's Posts live together, with a guarantee that one School's Editor can never touch another School's content.\\n\\n## Solution\\n\\nA small CMS in two halves, per ADR-001 and ADR-002.\\n\\n- A **local admin console** where the **Admin** (Jennifer) and **Editors** (one per School) manage **Posts** and **Pages**. Every Post and Page belongs to exactly one **School**; an Editor sees and changes only their own School's content.\\n- A **public static site**, produced by **Publish**, showing a merged news feed of all Schools' published Posts, a Schools index, a page per School, and a navigation built from published Pages.\\n\\nThe CMS holds no records about **Alumni**. Alumni accounts, approval, and per-field privacy are out of scope because the public site is static and the console is never on the internet.\\n\\nVocabulary is defined in `CONTEXT.md` and used throughout.\\n\\n## User Stories\\n\\n**Login and sessions**\\n1. As an Admin, I want to sign in with email and password, so that only authorised people can use the console.\\n2. As an Editor, I want to sign in and out, so that my School's content is protected on a shared computer.\\n3. As a User, I want a wrong password to be rejected with a plain message that does not say which part was wrong, so that accounts cannot be guessed.\\n4. As a User with a temporary password, I want to be made to choose my own password at first login, so that the Admin no longer knows it.\\n5. As a Deactivated User, I want my existing session to stop working immediately, so that a person who left cannot keep editing.\\n6. As an Admin, I want passwords stored only as argon2 hashes and never logged, so that a stolen database does not reveal them.\\n7. As a User, I want every form to carry a CSRF token, so that another website cannot make my browser change content.\\n\\n**Roles and Schools**\\n8. As an Admin, I want the 60 Schools loaded from a seed list with city and state, so that I do not have to type them in.\\n9. As an Admin, I want a Network School to exist alongside the 60, so that network-wide Pages like \\\"About\\\" and \\\"How to join\\\" have an owner.\\n10. As an Editor, I want to be assigned to exactly one School, so that my permissions match my job.\\n11. As an Editor, I want to see only my School's Posts and Pages, drafts included, so that I am not distracted by other Schools.\\n12. As an Editor, I want a request for another School's Post or Page to look like it does not exist, so that I cannot probe what other Schools have.\\n13. As an Admin, I want to act on every School's content, so that I can supervise and fix mistakes.\\n14. As an Admin, I want only me to be able to write to the Network School, so that network-wide Pages are not changed by an Editor.\\n\\n**User management (Admin only)**\\n15. As an Admin, I want to create Users and set a temporary password, so that Editors can start without email.\\n16. As an Admin, I want to choose a School for each new Editor, so that their scope is correct from the first login.\\n17. As an Admin, I want to change a User's role and reassign an Editor's School, so that staff changes are handled.\\n18. As an Admin, I want content to stay with its School when an Editor is reassigned, so that nothing silently moves.\\n19. As an Admin, I want to deactivate a User without deleting what they wrote, so that a School's history survives staff turnover.\\n20. As an Editor, I want user-management screens to be unavailable to me, so that I cannot create accounts or change roles.\\n\\n**Posts**\\n21. As an Editor, I want to create a Post with title, Markdown body, and Status, so that I can write news about my School.\\n22. As an Editor, I want the address of a Post generated from its title, so that I never have to think about slugs.\\n23. As an Editor, I want a duplicate address to be fixed for me by suggesting the next free one, so that I am not blocked by a rule I do not understand.\\n24. As an Editor, I want a reserved address to be refused with a plain message, so that I cannot break the site's fixed pages.\\n25. As an Editor, I want the Post to record who wrote it and when it was created and last changed, so that there is accountability.\\n26. As an Editor, I want to edit a published Post in place, so that I can fix a typo without taking it offline.\\n27. As an Editor, I want to delete my School's draft Posts, so that I can clean up.\\n28. As an Editor, I want to set a published Post back to draft instead of deleting it, so that I can withdraw it safely.\\n29. As an Admin, I want to permanently delete published content, so that removal of live content needs a second person.\\n30. As an Alumnus, I want to see a Post labelled by School and not by author name, so that individual staff are not exposed.\\n\\n**Pages**\\n31. As an Editor, I want to create and manage Pages for my School with the same rules as Posts, so that standing information is available.\\n32. As an Admin, I want to pin Network School Pages at the front of the navigation, so that \\\"About\\\" and \\\"How to join\\\" come first.\\n33. As an Alumnus, I want School Pages listed alphabetically after the pinned ones, so that I can find things predictably.\\n\\n**Admin console**\\n34. As an Editor, I want a dashboard with counts of my School's draft and published Posts and Pages, so that I see my School's state at a glance.\\n35. As an Admin, I want a dashboard for the whole Network with a per-School breakdown, so that I can see which Schools are not posting.\\n36. As an Admin, I want a count of items changed since the last Publish, so that I know whether Publish is needed.\\n37. As an Editor, I want a content list I can filter by Status and by type, so that I can find what to work on.\\n38. As an Admin, I want to filter the content list by School as well, so that I can look at one School.\\n39. As an Editor, I want a preview of my Markdown exactly as the public site will render it, sanitized, so that I know what alumni will see.\\n\\n**Publish and the public site**\\n40. As an Admin, I want to run Publish to export all Schools' published content at once, so that I control what goes live.\\n41. As an Editor, I want setting a Post's Status to published to mean \\\"ready\\\", not \\\"live\\\", so that I understand it appears at the next Publish.\\n42. As an Alumnus, I want a home page with a merged feed of every School's published Posts, newest first, so that I see the whole network in one place.\\n43. As an Alumnus, I want a Schools index listing all 60 Schools as plain text, so that I can find mine without using a map.\\n44. As an Alumnus, I want a page per School listing its published Posts and Pages, so that I can follow my own School.\\n45. As an Alumnus, I want the site to contain only published content, so that unfinished work is never visible.\\n46. As an Alumnus, I want unpublished or deleted items to disappear at the next Publish, so that withdrawn content stays withdrawn.\\n47. As an Alumnus, I want Markdown rendered safely, with scripts stripped, so that the site cannot attack me.\\n48. As an Alumnus, I want images linked from other sites to display in Posts, so that school news can have photos.\\n49. As an Alumnus, I want every link on the site to work when it is served from a subfolder, so that nothing is broken on the hosting platform.\\n\\n## Implementation Decisions\\n\\n**Domain model** (terms as in `CONTEXT.md`)\\n- **School**: name, short name, city, state, and a stable URL name. About 60 rows plus the **Network School**. Loaded from a seed list; there is no console screen to add, edit, or remove Schools.\\n- **User**: email, argon2 password hash, role (admin or editor), active flag, and a \\\"must change password\\\" flag. An Editor has exactly one School; an Admin has none.\\n- **Post** and **Page**: School, title, address (slug), Markdown body, Status (draft or published), author, created and updated timestamps. Pages that belong to the Network School also carry a pin order. A \\\"changed since last Publish\\\" comparison needs the time of the last Publish to be recorded.\\n- Slugs are unique per School, separately for Posts and for Pages.\\n\\n**Permissions** (enforced in data access, never only in templates)\\n- Editor queries are always filtered to their School. A Post or Page of another School returns not-found for every route (view, edit, delete, status change).\\n- Editors may create, edit, and delete their School's draft content, and may set published content back to draft. Only the Admin may permanently delete published content.\\n- Only the Admin may manage Users, reassign Schools, write Network School content, pin Pages, and run Publish.\\n- A User is checked against the database on each request, so deactivation and role changes take effect immediately.\\n- No route may rely on the client to name the School; the School comes from the signed-in User, or from the Admin's explicit choice.\\n\\n**Public site structure** (all paths relative)\\n- Home: merged feed of published Posts across Schools, newest first, each labelled with its School.\\n- Schools index: all 60 Schools as an alphabetical text list.\\n- Per-School page: that School's published Posts and Pages.\\n- Individual content at `<school>/posts/<slug>.html` and `<school>/pages/<slug>.html`, so a Post and a Page may share a slug within a School.\\n- Navigation: Pinned Pages first in the Admin's order, then the Schools entry. School Pages appear on the School's own page, not in the top-level nav, so 60 Schools do not clutter it.\\n- A reserved slug list protects fixed paths (the home, the Schools index, and the like).\\n- Byline shows the School only.\\n\\n**Publish**\\n- Rebuilds the entire output from scratch on every run (existing behaviour of the render function), writing only published rows. Removed or unpublished items disappear.\\n- Records the time of the last Publish for the dashboard.\\n- Markdown is sanitized before it is rendered anywhere (console preview and public). Script and event-handler content is stripped. External image links are allowed; relative-path rules apply only to the site's own links.\\n\\n**Console**\\n- Dashboards: Editor sees own-School counts (Posts and Pages, draft and published, changed since last Publish). Admin sees Network counts plus a per-School breakdown.\\n- Content list filters: Status and type for everyone; School for the Admin.\\n- Preview renders the same sanitized HTML as the public site.\\n- Forms carry CSRF tokens. Passwords are argon2-hashed; a new User must change a temporary password at first login.\\n- Plain-language error messages for slug problems (duplicate suggests the next free address; reserved is refused).\\n\\n**Sequencing hint for tickets**: each ticket should be a thin vertical slice (model, route, template, test) that is demoable alone, for example login and roles with a School-scoped list, then Post create and edit, then Page and nav, then Publish, then users, then dashboard and filters.\\n\\n## Testing Decisions\\n\\n- **Good tests assert external behaviour**: HTTP responses, redirects, what appears in the rendered pages, and the files Publish writes. They do not assert on internal functions or SQL.\\n- **Seam 1, HTTP**: the existing `client` and `client_as(role)` fixtures. Needs extending with a second School's Editor so cross-School rules are testable, and a seed with several Schools plus the Network School.\\n- **Seam 2, Publish**: run Publish against a seeded temporary database into a temporary directory, then read the generated files. Assertions: only published content present; drafts absent; sanitized Markdown; nav built from Pinned Pages; all href and src values relative; unpublished items removed on the next run.\\n- Required cross-School tests, per route (view, edit, delete, status change): an Editor of one School gets not-found on another's Post and Page.\\n- Prior art: `tests/test_t00.py` (HTTP smoke test) and the module docstring in `tests/conftest.py`.\\n- Read each test `/tdd` writes before letting it pass: a test written against a misunderstanding goes green.\\n\\n## Out of Scope\\n\\n- Alumni accounts, directory, approval, and per-field privacy (phone, email, occupation). The CMS stores no Alumni data.\\n- A US map of Schools. This is a non-functional mockup outside `site/`, with made-up data, only after the CMS works.\\n- Image uploads and a media library (external image links are allowed).\\n- Comments, search, tags, categories.\\n- Scheduled publishing and revision history.\\n- Rich-text editing and page builders (excluded by the assignment).\\n- Email, password reset by email, and any live deployment of the admin console.\\n- Adding, editing, or removing Schools from the console.\\n- Multi-site.\\n\\n## Further Notes\\n\\n- No new ADRs: nothing beyond ADR-001 and ADR-002 is both hard to reverse and a real trade-off. The Status-versus-Publish split lives here.\\n- Known consequences to explain to the client: a published item is live only at the next Publish; unpublishing breaks any old links to it; the public site is open to anyone (no reader login).\\n- Editing a published Post changes the stored copy immediately; there is no separate working copy.\\n- The seed file of 60 Schools needs real data from the client; use made-up placeholders for demos.\\n\"}\n.claude/hooks/ctx_compact_log.py\n.claude/hooks/ctx_guard.py\n.claude/hooks/ctx_statusline.py\n.claude/hooks/save_transcript.py\n.claude/settings.json\n.env.example\n.gitignore\nCLAUDE.md\nCONTEXT.md\nREADME.md\nSECURITY-CHECKLIST.md\napp/__init__.py\napp/cli.py\napp/db.py\napp/main.py\napp/markdown.py\napp/passwords.py\napp/posts.py\napp/publish.py\napp/routes/auth.py\napp/routes/console.py\napp/routes/posts.py\napp/security.py\napp/seed.py\napp/settings.py\napp/templating.py\ndocs/adr/ADR-001-local-admin-static-public.md\ndocs/adr/ADR-002-stack.md\ndocs/handoff/.gitkeep\ndocs/manual_iphs400_mp2-web-cms_20260922.md\ndocs/mp2-grading-rubric_20260922.md\ndocs/mp2-setup_context-threshold-hook_20260922.md\ndocs/process/.gitkeep\ndocs/screenshots/.gitkeep\nnotes/.gitkeep\nnotes/client-brief.md\nnotes/cms-field-notes.md\nnotes/cms-vocabulary-research.md\nnotes/token-budget-plan.md\nnotes/usage-ledger.csv\npyproject.toml\nscripts/check_submission.py\nscripts/seed_demo.py\nscripts/usage_report.py\ntemplates/admin/home.html\ntemplates/admin/layout.html\ntemplates/admin/post_form.html\ntemplates/admin/posts.html\ntemplates/base.html\ntemplates/change_password.html\ntemplates/login.html\ntemplates/public/home.html\ntests/__init__.py\ntests/conftest.py\ntests/test_ctx_guard.py\ntests/test_t00.py\ntests/test_t01.py\ntests/test_t02.py\ntests/test_usage_report.py\nuv.lock\n[project]\nname = \"iphs400-mp2-cms\"\nversion = \"0.1.0\"\ndescription = \"IPHS 400 MP2 — a small web CMS with a local admin console and a static public site\"\nrequires-python = \">=3.12\"\ndependencies = [\n = [\"app\"]\n# Domain Docs\n\nHow the engineering skills should consume this repo's domain documentation when exploring the codebase.\n\n## Before exploring, read these\n\n- **`CONTEXT.md`** at the repo root, or\n- **`CONTEXT-MAP.md`** at the repo root if it exists: it points at one `CONTEXT.md` per context. Read each one relevant to the topic.\n- **`docs/adr/`**: read ADRs that touch the area you're about to work in. In multi-context repos, also check `src/<context>/docs/adr/` for context-scoped decisions.\n\nIf any of these files don't exist, **proceed silently**. Don't flag their absence; don't suggest creating them upfront. The `/domain-modeling` skill (reached via `/grill-with-docs` and `/improve-codebase-architecture`) creates them lazily when terms or decisions actually get resolved.\n\n## File structure\n\nSingle-context repo (most repos):\n\n```\n/\n├── CONTEXT.md\n├── docs/adr/\n│   ├── 0001-event-sourced-orders.md\n│   └── 0002-postgres-for-write-model.md\n└── src/\n```\n\nMulti-context repo (presence of `CONTEXT-MAP.md` at the root):\n\n```\n/\n├── CONTEXT-MAP.md\n├── docs/adr/                          ← system-wide decisions\n└── src/\n    ├── ordering/\n    │   ├── CONTEXT.md\n    │   └── docs/adr/                  ← context-specific decisions\n    └── billing/\n        ├── CONTEXT.md\n        └── docs/adr/\n```\n\n## Use the glossary's vocabulary\n\nWhen your output names a domain concept (in an issue title, a refactor proposal, a hypothesis, a test name), use the term as defined in `CONTEXT.md`. Don't drift to synonyms the glossary explicitly avoids.\n\nIf the concept you need isn't in the glossary yet, that's a signal: either you're inventing language the project doesn't use (reconsider) or there's a real gap (note it for `/domain-modeling`).\n\n## Flag ADR conflicts\n\nIf your output contradicts an existing ADR, surface it explicitly rather than silently overriding:\n\n> _Contradicts ADR-0007 (event-sourced orders), but worth reopening because…_\n# Issue tracker: GitHub\n\nIssues and specs for this repo live as GitHub issues. Use the `gh` CLI for all operations.\n\n## Conventions\n\n- **Create an issue**: `gh issue create --title \"...\" --body \"...\"`. Use a heredoc for multi-line bodies.\n- **Read an issue**: `gh issue view <number> --comments`, filtering comments by `jq` and also fetching labels.\n- **List issues**: `gh issue list --state open --json number,title,body,labels,comments --jq '[.[] | {number, title, body, labels: [.labels[].name], comments: [.comments[].body]}]'` with appropriate `--label` and `--state` filters.","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"1ca97404-2b54-4598-b859-b64849ca86a4","timestamp":"2026-10-14T23:40:22.769Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"{\"body\":\"## Problem Statement\\n\\nThe Jesuit Schools Network has about 60 member high schools. Their alumni news is scattered across social media accounts and buried on each school's own news page, so alumni connect with classmates but not with the wider network. Each school has one person (usually the head of alumni relations) who writes about their school. The network director, Jennifer LaMaster, supervises but does not write day to day. She needs one place where every School's Posts live together, with a guarantee that one School's Editor can never touch another School's content.\\n\\n## Solution\\n\\nA small CMS in two halves, per ADR-001 and ADR-002.\\n\\n- A **local admin console** where the **Admin** (Jennifer) and **Editors** (one per School) manage **Posts** and **Pages**. Every Post and Page belongs to exactly one **School**; an Editor sees and changes only their own School's content.\\n- A **public static site**, produced by **Publish**, showing a merged news feed of all Schools' published Posts, a Schools index, a page per School, and a navigation built from published Pages.\\n\\nThe CMS holds no records about **Alumni**. Alumni accounts, approval, and per-field privacy are out of scope because the public site is static and the console is never on the internet.\\n\\nVocabulary is defined in `CONTEXT.md` and used throughout.\\n\\n## User Stories\\n\\n**Login and sessions**\\n1. As an Admin, I want to sign in with email and password, so that only authorised people can use the console.\\n2. As an Editor, I want to sign in and out, so that my School's content is protected on a shared computer.\\n3. As a User, I want a wrong password to be rejected with a plain message that does not say which part was wrong, so that accounts cannot be guessed.\\n4. As a User with a temporary password, I want to be made to choose my own password at first login, so that the Admin no longer knows it.\\n5. As a Deactivated User, I want my existing session to stop working immediately, so that a person who left cannot keep editing.\\n6. As an Admin, I want passwords stored only as argon2 hashes and never logged, so that a stolen database does not reveal them.\\n7. As a User, I want every form to carry a CSRF token, so that another website cannot make my browser change content.\\n\\n**Roles and Schools**\\n8. As an Admin, I want the 60 Schools loaded from a seed list with city and state, so that I do not have to type them in.\\n9. As an Admin, I want a Network School to exist alongside the 60, so that network-wide Pages like \\\"About\\\" and \\\"How to join\\\" have an owner.\\n10. As an Editor, I want to be assigned to exactly one School, so that my permissions match my job.\\n11. As an Editor, I want to see only my School's Posts and Pages, drafts included, so that I am not distracted by other Schools.\\n12. As an Editor, I want a request for another School's Post or Page to look like it does not exist, so that I cannot probe what other Schools have.\\n13. As an Admin, I want to act on every School's content, so that I can supervise and fix mistakes.\\n14. As an Admin, I want only me to be able to write to the Network School, so that network-wide Pages are not changed by an Editor.\\n\\n**User management (Admin only)**\\n15. As an Admin, I want to create Users and set a temporary password, so that Editors can start without email.\\n16. As an Admin, I want to choose a School for each new Editor, so that their scope is correct from the first login.\\n17. As an Admin, I want to deactivate a User without deleting what they wrote, so that a School's history survives staff turnover.\\n20. As an Editor, I want user-management screens to be unavailable to me, so that I cannot create accounts or change roles.\\n\\n**Posts**\\n21. As an Editor, I want to create a Post with title, Markdown body, and Status, so that I can write news about my School.\\n22. Assertions: only published content present; drafts absent; sanitized Markdown; nav built from Pinned Pages; all href and src values relative; unpublished items removed on the next run.\\n- Required cross-School tests, per route (view, edit, delete, status change): an Editor of one School gets not-found on another's Post and Page.\\n- Prior art: `tests/test_t00.py` (HTTP smoke test) and the module docstring in `tests/conftest.py`.\\n- Read each test `/tdd` writes before letting it pass: a test written against a misunderstanding goes green.\\n\\n## Out of Scope\\n\\n- Alumni accounts, directory, approval, and per-field privacy (phone, email, occupation). The CMS stores no Alumni data.\\n- A US map of Schools. This is a non-functional mockup outside `site/`, with made-up data, only after the CMS works.\\n- Image uploads and a media library (external image links are allowed).\\n- Comments, search, tags, categories.\\n- Scheduled publishing and revision history.\\n- Rich-text editing and page builders (excluded by the assignment).\\n- Email, password reset by email, and any live deployment of the admin console.\\n- Adding, editing, or removing Schools from the console.\\n- Multi-site.\\n\\n## Further Notes\\n\\n- No new ADRs: nothing beyond ADR-001 and ADR-002 is both hard to reverse and a real trade-off. The Status-versus-Publish split lives here.\\n- Known consequences to explain to the client: a published item is live only at the next Publish; unpublishing breaks any old links to it; the public site is open to anyone (no reader login).\\n- Editing a published Post changes the stored copy immediately; there is no separate working copy.\\n- The seed file of 60 Schools needs real data from the client; use made-up placeholders for demos.\\n\"}\n.claude/hooks/ctx_compact_log.py\n.claude/hooks/ctx_guard.py\n.claude/hooks/ctx_statusline.py\n.claude/hooks/save_transcript.py\n.claude/settings.json\n.env.example\n.gitignore\nCLAUDE.md\nCONTEXT.md\nREADME.md\nSECURITY-CHECKLIST.md\napp/__init__.py\napp/cli.py\napp/db.py\napp/main.py\napp/markdown.py\napp/passwords.py\napp/posts.py\napp/publish.py\napp/routes/auth.py\napp/routes/console.py\napp/routes/posts.py\napp/security.py\napp/seed.py\napp/settings.py\napp/templating.py\ndocs/adr/ADR-001-local-admin-static-public.md\ndocs/adr/ADR-002-stack.md\ndocs/handoff/.gitkeep\ndocs/manual_iphs400_mp2-web-cms_20260922.md\ndocs/mp2-grading-rubric_20260922.md\ndocs/mp2-setup_context-threshold-hook_20260922.md\ndocs/process/.gitkeep\ndocs/screenshots/.gitkeep\nnotes/.gitkeep\nnotes/client-brief.md\nnotes/cms-field-notes.md\nnotes/cms-vocabulary-research.md\nnotes/token-budget-plan.md\nnotes/usage-ledger.csv\npyproject.toml\nscripts/check_submission.py\nscripts/seed_demo.py\nscripts/usage_report.py\ntemplates/admin/home.html\ntemplates/admin/layout.html\ntemplates/admin/post_form.html\ntemplates/admin/posts.html\ntemplates/base.html\ntemplates/change_password.html\ntemplates/login.html\ntemplates/public/home.html\ntests/__init__.py\ntests/conftest.py\ntests/test_ctx_guard.py\ntests/test_t00.py\ntests/test_t01.py\ntests/test_t02.py\ntests/test_usage_report.py\nuv.lock\n[project]\nname = \"iphs400-mp2-cms\"\nversion = \"0.1.0\"\ndescription = \"IPHS 400 MP2 — a small web CMS with a local admin console and a static public site\"\nrequires-python = \">=3.12\"\ndependencies = [\n    \"fastapi>=0.115\",\n    \"uvicorn[standard]>=0.32\",\n    \"jinja2>=3.1\",\n    \"python-multipart>=0.0.12\",   # form posts\n    \"itsdangerous>=2.2\",          # signed session cookies\n    \"argon2-cffi>=23.1\",          # password hashing (never store plain passwords)\n    \"markdown-it-py>=3.0\",        # Markdown -> HTML\n    \"nh3>=0.2\",                   # HTML sanitizer (strips <script> etc.)\n    \"ghp-import>=2.1\",            # push site/ to the gh-pages branch\n]\n\n[dependency-groups]\ndev = [\"pytest>=8.3\", \"httpx>=0.28\"]\n\n[project.scripts]\ncms = \"app.cli:main\"\n\n[tool.pytest.ini_options]\ntestpaths = [\"tests\"]\n\n[build-system]\nrequires = [\"hatchling\"]\nbuild-backend = \"hatchling.build\"\n\n[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel]\npackages = [\"app\"]\n# Domain Docs\n\nHow the engineering skills should consume this repo's domain documentation when exploring the codebase.\n\n## Before exploring, read these\n\n- **`CONTEXT.md`** at the repo root, or\n- **`CONTEXT-MAP.md`** at the repo root if it exists: it points at one `CONTEXT.md` per context. Read each one relevant to the topic.\n- **`docs/adr/`**: read ADRs that touch the area you're about to work in. In multi-context repos, also check `src/<context>/docs/adr/` for context-scoped decisions.\n\nIf any of these files don't exist, **proceed silently**. Don't flag their absence; don't suggest creating them upfront. The `/domain-modeling` skill (reached via `/grill-with-docs` and `/improve-codebase-architecture`) creates them lazily when terms or decisions actually get resolved.\n\n## File structure\n\nSingle-context repo (most repos):\n\n```\n/\n├── CONTEXT.md\n├── docs/adr/\n│   ├── 0001-event-sourced-orders.md\n│   └── 0002-postgres-for-write-model.md\n└── src/\n```\n\nMulti-context repo (presence of `CONTEXT-MAP.md` at the root):\n\n```\n/\n├── CONTEXT-MAP.md\n├── docs/adr/                          ← system-wide decisions\n└── src/\n    ├── ordering/\n    │   ├── CONTEXT.md\n    │   └── docs/adr/                  ← context-specific decisions\n    └── billing/\n        ├── CONTEXT.md\n        └── docs/adr/\n```\n\n## Use the glossary's vocabulary\n\nWhen your output names a domain concept (in an issue title, a refactor proposal, a hypothesis, a test name), use the term as defined in `CONTEXT.md`. Don't drift to synonyms the glossary explicitly avoids.\n\nIf the concept you need isn't in the glossary yet, that's a signal: either you're inventing language the project doesn't use (reconsider) or there's a real gap (note it for `/domain-modeling`).\n\n## Flag ADR conflicts\n\nIf your output contradicts an existing ADR, surface it explicitly rather than silently overriding:\n\n> _Contradicts ADR-0007 (event-sourced orders), but worth reopening because…_\n# Issue tracker: GitHub\n\nIssues and specs for this repo live as GitHub issues. Use the `gh` CLI for all operations.\n\n## Conventions\n\n- **Create an issue**: `gh issue create --title \"...\" --body \"...\"`. Use a heredoc for multi-line bodies.\n- **Read an issue**: `gh issue view <number> --comments`, filtering comments by `jq` and also fetching labels.\n- **List issues**: `gh issue list --state open --json number,title,body,labels,comments --jq '[.[] | {number, title, body, labels: [.labels[].name], comments: [.comments[].body]}]'` with appropriate `--label` and `--state` filters.","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"31c9b568-8a92-4999-814e-90da1e2e8cc1","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":2,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":42},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"joehaugh/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"joehaugh/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"joehaugh/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"wsl"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3ba7d7ac-7ce3-4eba-adef-252d3ac044c7","session_id":"86c96d58-6503-4970-83eb-16ca927e0436","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"c99fff69-e931-4b00-ba0e-51f2c392c118","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"480a26b4-0116-44a6-9e8f-c3f6c82de233","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"d5cad169-0b32-44c0-b6a6-254151a3c343","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QRHZk9YeZEDPuEkjF9V7vZ","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return out\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nThe admin console lives under /admin (sign-in at /login) and the public site\npreview at /. Routes live in their own modules under app/routes/ and are\nincluded here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom contextlib import asynccontextmanager\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Request\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.db import init_db\nfrom app.routes import auth, console, posts\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\n\n@asynccontextmanager\nasync def lifespan(app: FastAPI):\n    init_db()\n    yield\n\n\ndef create_app() -> FastAPI:\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\", lifespan=lifespan)\n    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       same_site=\"lax\", session_cookie=\"cms_session\")\n    app.include_router(auth.router)\n    app.include_router(console.router)\n    app.include_router(posts.router)\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Later tickets: add routers to app/routes/ and include them above.\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n\"\"\"Markdown to sanitized HTML. Every place Markdown is rendered goes through here.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport nh3\nfrom markdown_it import MarkdownIt\n\n_md = MarkdownIt(\"commonmark\", {\"html\": True})\n\n\ndef render_markdown(source: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Render, then strip scripts, event handlers and unsafe URL schemes.\"\"\"\n    return nh3.clean(_md.render(source))\n\"\"\"SQLite access. The path is read at call time so tests can point it elsewhere.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom collections.abc import Iterator\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schools (\n    id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    name        TEXT NOT NULL,\n    short_name  TEXT NOT NULL,\n    city        TEXT NOT NULL,\n    state       TEXT NOT NULL,\n    url_name    TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    is_network  INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id                    INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    email                 TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    password_hash         TEXT NOT NULL,\n    role                  TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active                INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    must_change_password  INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    session_epoch         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,  -- bumped to void old cookies\n    school_id             INTEGER REFERENCES schools(id),\n    CHECK ((role = 'editor') = (school_id IS NOT NULL))\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (\n    id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    school_id   INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES schools(id),\n    title       TEXT NOT NULL,\n    slug        TEXT NOT NULL,\n    body        TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status      TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id   INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),\n    created_at  TEXT NOT NULL,\n    updated_at  TEXT NOT NULL,\n    UNIQUE (school_id, slug)\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect() -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(conn: sqlite3.Connection | None = None) -> None:\n    own = conn is None\n    conn = conn or connect()\n    conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n    conn.commit()\n    if own:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef get_db() -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    \"\"\"FastAPI dependency: one connection per request.\"\"\"\n    conn = connect()\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\"\"\"Console routes. Every route here requires a signed-in User.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Request\n\nfrom app.security import current_user\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\", dependencies=[Depends(current_user)])\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef home(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/home.html\", {\"title\": \"Admin\", \"user\": user})\n\"\"\"Session-backed CSRF tokens and the signed-in-user dependency.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hmac\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom markupsafe import Markup\n\nfrom app.db import get_db\n\nCSRF_FIELD = \"csrf_token\"\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(CSRF_FIELD)\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[CSRF_FIELD] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\ndef csrf_field(request: Request) -> Markup:\n    \"\"\"Hidden input for every state-changing form: {{ csrf_field(request) }}.\"\"\"\n    return Markup('<input type=\"hidden\" name=\"{}\" value=\"{}\">').format(\n        CSRF_FIELD, csrf_token(request))\n\n\ndef verify_csrf(request: Request, csrf_token: str = Form(\"\", alias=CSRF_FIELD)) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Dependency for every POST route; rejects a missing or wrong token.\"\"\"\n    expected = request.session.get(CSRF_FIELD, \"\")\n    if not expected or not hmac.compare_digest(expected, csrf_token):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")\n\n\ndef redirect_to(path: str) -> HTTPException:\n    return HTTPException(status_code=303, headers={\"Location\": path})\n\n\ndef _load_user(request: Request, db: sqlite3.Connection) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    \"\"\"The signed-in User, re-read from the database on every request.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    user = db.execute(\n        \"SELECT users.*, schools.name AS school_name FROM users\"\n        \" LEFT JOIN schools ON schools.id = users.school_id\"\n        \" WHERE users.id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone() if user_id else None\n    if (user is None or not user[\"active\"]\n            or request.session.get(\"epoch\") != user[\"session_epoch\"]):\n        request.session.clear()\n        raise redirect_to(\"/login\")\n    return user\n\n\ndef end_sessions(db: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Void every cookie issued so far for this User; returns the new epoch.\"\"\"\n    db.execute(\"UPDATE users SET session_epoch = session_epoch + 1 WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,))\n    db.commit()\n    return db.execute(\"SELECT session_epoch FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()[0]\n\n\ndef current_user_allow_pending(request: Request, db: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    return _load_user(request, db)\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request, db: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    \"\"\"Console routes depend on this: signed in, active, and past the forced change.\"\"\"\n    user = _load_user(request, db)\n    if user[\"must_change_password\"]:\n        raise redirect_to(\"/change-password\")\n    return user\n<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>{% block title %}{{ title }}{% endblock %}</title>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"{{ css_path | default('style.css') }}\">\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header><a href=\"{{ home_path | default('index.html') }}\">{{ title }}</a></header>\n  <main>{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>\n  <footer><small>IPHS 400 Mini-Project #2</small></footer>\n</body>\n</html>\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  {% if items %}\n    <ul>{% for item in items %}<li><a href=\"{{ item.href }}\">{{ item.title }}</a></li>{% endfor %}</ul>\n  {% else %}\n    <p>Nothing published yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block body %}\n  <h1>Console</h1>\n  <dl>\n    <dt>Role</dt><dd>{{ \"Admin\" if user.role == \"admin\" else \"Editor\" }}</dd>\n Passwords come from the environment in\n# scripts/seed_demo.py; in tests they are fixed and meaningless.\n# \"editor\" and \"editor_b\" belong to different Schools, for cross-School tests.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n    \"editor_b\": {\"email\": \"editor-b@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-b-pw\"},\n}\n\n_CSRF = re.compile(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"')\n\n\n@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)\ndef cheap_hashing(monkeypatch):\n    \"\"\"Same argon2 algorithm, minimal cost, so seeding users per test stays fast.\"\"\"\n    from argon2 import PasswordHasher\n    from app import passwords\n\n    hasher = PasswordHasher(time_cost=1, memory_cost=8, parallelism=1)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(passwords, \"_hasher\", hasher)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(passwords, \"_DUMMY_HASH\", hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\"))\n\n\n@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)\ndef db(tmp_path, monkeypatch):\n    \"\"\"A fresh seeded database for every test; returns an open connection.\"\"\"\n    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, \"DATABASE_PATH\", tmp_path / \"test.db\")\n    conn = connect()\n    init_db(conn)\n    seed(conn, {key: u[\"password\"] for key, u in DEMO_USERS.items()})\n    yield conn\n    conn.close()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client() -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(create_app())\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef csrf():\n    \"\"\"csrf(client, path) -> the CSRF token from the form served at `path`.\"\"\"\n\n    def _token(c: TestClient, path: str = \"/login\") -> str:\n        match = _CSRF.search(c.get(path).text)\n        assert match, f\"No CSRF token in the form at {path}\"\n        return match.group(1)\n\n    return _token\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as(csrf):\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app())\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"],\n                                          \"csrf_token\": csrf(c)},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n\"\"\"Seed data: Schools, the Network School, an Admin, and Editors for two Schools.\n\nThe School rows are made-up placeholders; the real list of about 60 comes from\nthe client. Safe to run twice.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app.passwords import hash_password\n\nNETWORK_URL_NAME = \"network\"\n\nSCHOOLS = [\n    # name, short_name, city, state, url_name, is_network\n    (\"Jesuit Schools Network\", \"Network\", \"Washington\", \"DC\", NETWORK_URL_NAME, 1),\n    (\"St. Ignatius Preparatory School\", \"St. Ignatius\", \"Springfield\", \"OH\", \"st-ignatius\", 0),\n    (\"Loyola Academy of Riverton\", \"Loyola Riverton\", \"Riverton\", \"IL\", \"loyola-riverton\", 0),\n    (\"Xavier High School of Lakeside\", \"Xavier Lakeside\", \"Lakeside\", \"MI\", \"xavier-lakeside\", 0),\n    (\"Campion College Preparatory\", \"Campion\", \"Prairie Falls\", \"WI\", \"campion\", 0),\n]\n\n# key -> (email, role, school url_name)\nUSERS = {\n    \"admin\": (\"admin@example.test\", \"admin\", None),\n    \"editor\": (\"editor@example.test\", \"editor\", \"st-ignatius\"),\n    \"editor_b\": (\"editor-b@example.test\", \"editor\", \"loyola-riverton\"),\n}\n\n\ndef seed(conn: sqlite3.Connection, passwords: dict[str, str]) -> None:\n    \"\"\"`passwords` maps the keys in USERS to a plain password (hashed here).\"\"\"\n    for row in SCHOOLS:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT OR IGNORE INTO schools (name, short_name, city, state, url_name, is_network)\"\n            \" VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT OR IGNORE INTO users (email, password_hash, role, school_id)\"\n            \" VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (email, hash_password(passwords[key]), role, school[\"id\"] if school else None))\n    conn.commit()\n\"\"\"Configuration, read from the environment (never hard-code secrets).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport secrets\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nTEMPLATES = ROOT / \"templates\"\nSITE = ROOT / \"site\"\n\n# No built-in default: a publicly known key would let anyone forge a session.\n# Unset means a random key per process (sessions end when the server restarts).\nSECRET_KEY = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SECRET_KEY\") or secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\nDATABASE_PATH = Path(os.environ.get(\"CMS_DATABASE\", ROOT / \"cms.db\"))\nSITE_TITLE = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SITE_TITLE\", \"My CMS\")\n# Set this to your Pages URL once you deploy, e.g.\n# \"\")","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"c244ebe9-ea7f-4095-9572-16d7b8ba1363","timestamp":"2026-10-14T23:40:25.122Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return out\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nThe admin console lives under /admin (sign-in at /login) and the public site\npreview at /. Routes live in their own modules under app/routes/ and are\nincluded here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom contextlib import asynccontextmanager\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Request\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.db import init_db\nfrom app.routes import auth, console, posts\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\n\n@asynccontextmanager\nasync def lifespan(app: FastAPI):\n    init_db()\n    yield\n\n\ndef create_app() -> FastAPI:\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\", lifespan=lifespan)\n    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       same_site=\"lax\", session_cookie=\"cms_session\")\n    app.include_router(auth.router)\n    app.include_router(console.router)\n    app.include_router(posts.router)\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Later tickets: add routers to app/routes/ and include them above.\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n\"\"\"Markdown to sanitized HTML. Every place Markdown is rendered goes through here.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport nh3\nfrom markdown_it import MarkdownIt\n\n_md = MarkdownIt(\"commonmark\", {\"html\": True})\n\n\ndef render_markdown(source: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Render, then strip scripts, event handlers and unsafe URL schemes.\"\"\"\n    return nh3.clean(_md.render(source))\n\"\"\"SQLite access. The path is read at call time so tests can point it elsewhere.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom collections.abc import Iterator\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schools (\n    id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    name        TEXT NOT NULL,\n    short_name  TEXT NOT NULL,\n    city        TEXT NOT NULL,\n    state       TEXT NOT NULL,\n    url_name    TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    is_network  INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id                    INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    email                 TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    password_hash         TEXT NOT NULL,\n    role                  TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active                INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    must_change_password  INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    session_epoch         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,  -- bumped to void old cookies\n    school_id             INTEGER REFERENCES schools(id),\n    CHECK ((role = 'editor') = (school_id IS NOT NULL))\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (\n    id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    school_id   INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES schools(id),\n    title       TEXT NOT NULL,\n    slug        TEXT NOT NULL,\n    body        TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status      TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id   INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),\n    created_at  TEXT NOT NULL,\n    updated_at  TEXT NOT NULL,\n    UNIQUE (school_id, slug)\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect() -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(conn: sqlite3.Connection | None = None) -> None:\n    own = conn is None\n    conn = conn or connect()\n    conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n    conn.commit()\n    if own:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef get_db() -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    \"\"\"FastAPI dependency: one connection per request.\"\"\"\n    conn = connect()\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\"\"\"Console routes. Every route here requires a signed-in User.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Request\n\nfrom app.security import current_user\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\", dependencies=[Depends(current_user)])\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef home(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/home.html\", {\"title\": \"Admin\", \"user\": user})\n\"\"\"Session-backed CSRF tokens and the signed-in-user dependency.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hmac\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom markupsafe import Markup\n\nfrom app.db import get_db\n\nCSRF_FIELD = \"csrf_token\"\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(CSRF_FIELD)\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[CSRF_FIELD] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\ndef csrf_field(request: Request) -> Markup:\n    \"\"\"Hidden input for every state-changing form: {{ csrf_field(request) }}.\"\"\"\n    return Markup('<input type=\"hidden\" name=\"{}\" value=\"{}\">').format(\n        CSRF_FIELD, csrf_token(request))\n\n\ndef verify_csrf(request: Request, csrf_token: str = Form(\"\", alias=CSRF_FIELD)) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Dependency for every POST route; rejects a missing or wrong token.\"\"\"\n    expected = request.session.get(CSRF_FIELD, \"\")\n    if not expected or not hmac.compare_digest(expected, csrf_token):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")\n\n\ndef redirect_to(path: str) -> HTTPException:\n    return HTTPException(status_code=303, headers={\"Location\": path})\n\n\ndef _load_user(request: Request, db: sqlite3.Connection) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    \"\"\"The signed-in User, re-read from the database on every request.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    user = db.execute(\n        \"SELECT users.*, schools.name AS school_name FROM users\"\n        \" LEFT JOIN schools ON schools.id = users.school_id\"\n        \" WHERE users.id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone() if user_id else None\n    if (user is None or not user[\"active\"]\n            or request.session.get(\"epoch\") != user[\"session_epoch\"]):\n        request.session.clear()\n        raise redirect_to(\"/login\")\n    return user\n\n\ndef end_sessions(db: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Void every cookie issued so far for this User; returns the new epoch.\"\"\"\n    db.execute(\"UPDATE users SET session_epoch = session_epoch + 1 WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,))\n    db.commit()\n    return db.execute(\"SELECT session_epoch FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()[0]\n\n\ndef current_user_allow_pending(request: Request, db: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    return _load_user(request, db)\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request, db: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    \"\"\"Console routes depend on this: signed in, active, and past the forced change.\"\"\"\n    user = _load_user(request, db)\n    if user[\"must_change_password\"]:\n        raise redirect_to(\"/change-password\")\n    return {"parentUuid":"a3e9c2d0-1828-41d1-addb-fa146e82813f","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"d5cad169-0b32-44c0-b6a6-254151a3c343","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017DJXG9wc1W3yzzMu6FCoYg","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Post data access. Scoping lives here, never only in templates.\n\nAn Editor's queries are always filtered to their own School, so another\nSchool's Post is simply absent (None). An Admin sees every School.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n\nSTATUSES = (\"draft\", \"published\")\n\n# Fixed paths of the public site that a Post address must not collide with.\nRESERVED_SLUGS = frozenset({\n    \"index\", \"schools\", \"posts\", \"pages\", \"style\", \"admin\", \"login\", \"logout\", \"new\",\n})\n\n\nclass PostError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"A problem the user can fix; the message is shown as written.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef slugify(text: str) -> str:\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", text.lower()).strip(\"-\")\n\n\ndef _now() -> str:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\")\n\n\ndef _scope(user) -> tuple[str, tuple]:\n    if user[\"role\"] == \"admin\":\n        return \"\", ()\n    return \" AND posts.school_id = ?\", (user[\"school_id\"],)\n\n\ndef list_posts(db: sqlite3.Connection, user) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    clause, params = _scope(user)\n    return db.execute(\n        \"SELECT posts.*, schools.short_name AS school_name FROM posts\"\n        \" JOIN schools ON schools.id = posts.school_id\"\n        f\" WHERE 1 = 1{clause} ORDER BY posts.updated_at DESC, posts.id DESC\", params).fetchall()\n\n\ndef get_post(db: sqlite3.Connection, user, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    clause, params = _scope(user)\n    return db.execute(\n        f\"SELECT posts.* FROM posts WHERE posts.id = ?{clause}\", (post_id, *params)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_schools(db: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    return db.execute(\"SELECT id, name FROM schools ORDER BY is_network DESC, name\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef target_school_id(db: sqlite3.Connection, user, chosen: str) -> int:\n    \"\"\"The Editor's own School; for an Admin, the School they explicitly chose.\"\"\"\n    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        return user[\"school_id\"]\n    if chosen.isdigit() and db.execute(\n            \"SELECT 1 FROM schools WHERE id = ?\", (int(chosen),)).fetchone():\n        return int(chosen)\n    raise PostError(\"Choose which School this Post belongs to.\")\n\n\ndef _clean(title: str, status: str) -> tuple[str, str]:\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise PostError(\"Give the Post a title.\")\n    if status not in STATUSES:\n        raise PostError(\"Status must be draft or published.\")\n    return title, status\n\n\ndef _free_slug(db, school_id: int, wanted: str) -> str:\n    slug = slugify(wanted)\n    if not slug:\n        raise PostError(\"The address needs at least one letter or number.\")\n    if slug in RESERVED_SLUGS:\n        raise PostError(f\"The address “{slug}” is reserved for a fixed page of the \"\n                        \"site. Please change the title or type a different address.\")\n    candidate, n = slug, 1\n    while db.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE school_id = ? AND slug = ?\",\n                     (school_id, candidate)).fetchone():\n        n += 1\n        candidate = f\"{slug}-{n}\"\n    return candidate\n\n\ndef create_post(db, user, *, school_id: int, title: str, body: str, status: str,\n                address: str) -> int:\n    title, status = _clean(title, status)\n    slug = _free_slug(db, school_id, address.strip() or title)\n    now = _now()\n    cur = db.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO posts (school_id, title, slug, body, status, author_id, created_at,\"\n        \" updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (school_id, title, slug, body, status, user[\"id\"], now, now))\n    db.commit()\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef update_post(db, post: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str, body: str, status: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"The address is fixed at creation: renaming it would break links to a live Post.\"\"\"\n    title, status = _clean(title, status)\n    db.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, body = ?, status = ?, updated_at = ?\"\n               \" WHERE id = ?\", (title, body, status, _now(), post[\"id\"]))\n    db.commit()\n\"\"\"Post routes: list, create, edit, preview. Requires a signed-in User.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import posts as store\nfrom app.db import get_db\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.security import current_user, verify_csrf\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/posts\", dependencies=[Depends(current_user)])\n\n\ndef _form(request, db, user, *, values, post=None, error=None, preview=None, status=200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, \"admin/post_form.html\", {\n        \"title\": \"Edit Post\" if post else \"New Post\", \"user\": user, \"post\": post,\n        \"values\": values, \"error\": error, \"preview_html\": preview,\n        \"schools\": store.list_schools(db) if user[\"role\"] == \"admin\" else [],\n    }, status_code=status)\n\n\ndef _own_or_404(db, user, post_id: int):\n    post = store.get_post(db, user, post_id)\n    if post is None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"Post not found.\")\n    return post\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef list_view(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), db=Depends(get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, \"admin/posts.html\", {\n        \"title\": \"Posts\", \"user\": user, \"posts\": store.list_posts(db, user)})\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef new_view(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), db=Depends(get_db)):\n    return _form(request, db, user, values={\"status\": \"draft\"})\n\n\n@router.post(\"/preview\", dependencies=[Depends(verify_csrf)])\ndef preview_new(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), db=Depends(get_db),\n                title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"), status: str = Form(\"draft\"),\n                address: str = Form(\"\"), school_id: str = Form(\"\")):\n    values = dict(title=title, body=body, status=status, address=address, school_id=school_id)\n    return _form(request, db, user, values=values, preview=render_markdown(body))\n\n\n@router.post(\"\", dependencies=[Depends(verify_csrf)])\ndef create(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), db: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db),\n           title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"), status: str = Form(\"draft\"),\n           address: str = Form(\"\"), school_id: str = Form(\"\")):\n    values = dict(title=title, body=body, status=status, address=address, school_id=school_id)\n    try:\n        target = store.target_school_id(db, user, school_id)\n        store.create_post(db, user, school_id=target, title=title, body=body,\n                          status=status, address=address)\n    except store.PostError as err:\n        return _form(request, db, user, values=values, error=str(err), status=400)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/edit\")\ndef edit_view(post_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), db=Depends(get_db)):\n    post = _own_or_404(db, user, post_id)\n    return _form(request, db, user, post=post, values=dict(post))\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/preview\", dependencies=[Depends(verify_csrf)])\ndef preview_existing(post_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(current_user),\n                     db=Depends(get_db), title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                     status: str = Form(\"draft\")):\n    post = _own_or_404(db, user, post_id)\n    values = dict(title=title, body=body, status=status, slug=post[\"slug\"])\n    return _form(request, db, user, post=post, values=values, (title,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef test_editor_creates_a_post_with_author_and_timestamps(client_as, csrf, db):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = create(c, csrf, status=\"published\")\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    row = post_row(db, \"Hello Alumni\")\n    assert row[\"school_id\"] == school_id(db, \"st-ignatius\")\n    assert row[\"status\"] == \"published\" and Scoping lives here, never only in templates.\n\nAn Editor's queries are always filtered to their own School, so another\nSchool's Post is simply absent (None). An Admin sees every School.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n\nSTATUSES = (\"draft\", \"published\")\n\n# Fixed paths of the public site that a Post address must not collide with.\nRESERVED_SLUGS = frozenset({\n    \"index\", \"schools\", \"posts\", \"pages\", \"style\", \"admin\", \"login\", \"logout\", \"new\",\n})\n\n\nclass PostError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"A problem the user can fix; the message is shown as written.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef slugify(text: str) -> str:\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", text.lower()).strip(\"-\")\n\n\ndef _now() -> str:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\")\n\n\ndef _scope(user) -> tuple[str, tuple]:\n    if user[\"role\"] == \"admin\":\n        return \"\", ()\n    return \" AND posts.school_id = ?\", (user[\"school_id\"],)\n\n\ndef list_posts(db: sqlite3.Connection, user) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    clause, params = _scope(user)\n    return db.execute(\n        \"SELECT posts.*, schools.short_name AS school_name FROM posts\"\n        \" JOIN schools ON schools.id = posts.school_id\"\n        f\" WHERE 1 = 1{clause} ORDER BY posts.updated_at DESC, posts.id DESC\", params).fetchall()\n\n\ndef get_post(db: sqlite3.Connection, user, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    clause, params = _scope(user)\n    return db.execute(\n        f\"SELECT posts.* FROM posts WHERE posts.id = ?{clause}\", (post_id, *params)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_schools(db: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    return db.execute(\"SELECT id, name FROM schools ORDER BY is_network DESC, name\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef target_school_id(db: sqlite3.Connection, user, chosen: str) -> int:\n    \"\"\"The Editor's own School; for an Admin, the School they explicitly chose.\"\"\"\n    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        return user[\"school_id\"]\n    if chosen.isdigit() and db.execute(\n            \"SELECT 1 FROM schools WHERE id = ?\", (int(chosen),)).fetchone():\n        return int(chosen)\n    raise PostError(\"Choose which School this Post belongs to.\")\n\n\ndef _clean(title: str, status: str) -> tuple[str, str]:\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise PostError(\"Give the Post a title.\")\n    if status not in STATUSES:\n        raise PostError(\"Status must be draft or published.\")\n    return title, status\n\n\ndef _free_slug(db, school_id: int, wanted: str) -> str:\n    slug = slugify(wanted)\n    if not slug:\n        raise PostError(\"The address needs at least one letter or number.\")\n    if slug in RESERVED_SLUGS:\n        raise PostError(f\"The address “{slug}” is reserved for a fixed page of the \"\n                        \"site. Please change the title or type a different address.\")\n    candidate, n = slug, 1\n    while db.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE school_id = ? AND slug = ?\",\n                     (school_id, candidate)).fetchone():\n        n += 1\n        candidate = f\"{slug}-{n}\"\n    return candidate\n\n\ndef create_post(db, user, *, school_id: int, title: str, body: str, status: str,\n                address: str) -> int:\n    title, status = _clean(title, status)\n    slug = _free_slug(db, school_id, address.strip() or title)\n    now = _now()\n    cur = db.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO posts (school_id, title, slug, body, status, author_id, created_at,\"\n        \" updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (school_id, title, slug, body, status, user[\"id\"], now, now))\n    db.commit()\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef update_post(db, post: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str, body: str, status: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"The address is fixed at creation: renaming it would break links to a live Post.\"\"\"\n    title, status = _clean(title, status)\n    db.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, body = ?, status = ?, updated_at = ?\"\n               \" WHERE id = ?\", (title, body, status, _now(), post[\"id\"]))\n    db.commit()\n\"\"\"Post routes: list, create, edit, preview. Requires a signed-in User.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import posts as store\nfrom app.db import get_db\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.security import current_user, verify_csrf\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/posts\", dependencies=[Depends(current_user)])\n\n\ndef _form(request, db, user, *, values, post=None, error=None, preview=None, status=200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, \"admin/post_form.html\", {\n        \"title\": \"Edit Post\" if post else \"New Post\", \"user\": user, \"post\": post,\n        \"values\": values, \"error\": error, \"preview_html\": preview,\n        \"schools\": store.list_schools(db) if user[\"role\"] == \"admin\" else [],\n    }, status_code=status)\n\n\ndef _own_or_404(db, user, post_id: int):\n    post = store.get_post(db, user, post_id)\n    if post is None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"Post not found.\")\n    return post\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef list_view(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), db=Depends(get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, \"admin/posts.html\", {\n        \"title\": \"Posts\", \"user\": user, \"posts\": store.list_posts(db, user)})\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef new_view(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), db=Depends(get_db)):\n    return _form(request, db, user, values={\"status\": \"draft\"})\n\n\n@router.post(\"/preview\", dependencies=[Depends(verify_csrf)])\ndef preview_new(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), db=Depends(get_db),\n                title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"), status: str = Form(\"draft\"),\n                address: str = Form(\"\"), school_id: str = Form(\"\")):\n    values = dict(title=title, body=body, status=status, address=address, school_id=school_id)\n    return _form(request, db, user, values=values, preview=render_markdown(body))\n\n\n@router.post(\"\", dependencies=[Depends(verify_csrf)])\ndef create(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), db: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db),\n           title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"), status: str = Form(\"draft\"),\n           address: str = Form(\"\"), school_id: str = Form(\"\")):\n    values = dict(title=title, body=body, status=status, address=address, school_id=school_id)\n    try:\n        target = store.target_school_id(db, user, school_id)\n        store.create_post(db, user, school_id=target, title=title, body=body,\n                          status=status, address=address)\n    except store.PostError as err:\n        return _form(request, db, user, values=values, error=str(err), status=400)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/edit\")\ndef edit_view(post_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), db=Depends(get_db)):\n    post = _own_or_404(db, user, post_id)\n    return _form(request, db, user, post=post, values=dict(post))\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/preview\", dependencies=[Depends(verify_csrf)])\ndef preview_existing(post_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(current_user),\n                     db=Depends(get_db), title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                     status: str = Form(\"draft\")):\n    post = _own_or_404(db, user, post_id)\n    values = dict(title=title, body=body, status=status, slug=post[\"slug\"])\n    return _form(request, db, user, post=post, values=values, preview=render_markdown(body))\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}\", dependencies=[Depends(verify_csrf)])\ndef update(post_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), db=Depends(get_db),\n           title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"), status: str = Form(\"draft\")):\n    post = _own_or_404(db, user, post_id)\n    values = dict(title=title, body=body, status=status, slug=post[\"slug\"])\n    try:\n        store.update_post(db, post, title=title, body=body, status=status)\n    except store.PostError as err:\n        return _form(request, db, user, post=post, status=\"draft\",\n           address=\"\", **extra):\n    data = {\"title\": title, \"body\": body, \"status\": status, \"address\": address,\n            \"csrf_token\": csrf(c,
